# PacEdu Form 5 English — 460 Daily Printable Worksheets
**Status:** ORIGINAL PACEDU DRAFT — PENDING_TEACHER_REVIEW — PENDING_OFFICIAL_INDICATOR_VERIFICATION. Not a Ministry-issued scheme or worksheet pack. No official indicator IDs, exact wording, source pages or approved sequence are claimed.
115 learning days across 23 planning weeks (Term 1: 9 weeks; Term 2: 9 weeks; Term 3: 5 weeks). Four differentiated pathways share the same objective. Form 5, Form 6 and Form 7 syllabus outcomes, prescribed texts, examination specifications and assessment criteria must be verified separately against the school's current authorised documents. Do not assume the Form 3–4 RSEC guide applies unchanged to senior forms.
Daily record: school-local date ____ | term/week/day ____ | official outcome/indicator + edition/page (after verification) ____ | evidence ____ | next step ____.
Select one day and one pathway in the dedicated viewer. School calendar controls teaching days. School IANA timezone supplies local print date/time; missing configuration must show a warning. Learner: __________ School: __________ Teacher: __________
Accept appropriate accessible response modes. Credit accurate synonyms, paraphrase and evidence-supported interpretations. Do not penalise accent or disability-related speech differences.

---

## Day 1 · Term 1 Week 1 Day 1 · Copper
**Theme:** Identity, language and society | **Area:** Listening and speaking
**Objective:** Analyse how language, identity and context shape meaning; develop a close reading and analytical response.
**Task:** Present a reasoned position and respond thoughtfully to questions. Use the teacher-selected authorised text. Identify the central claim or idea, select one supporting detail, and explain one implication or question.
**Copper:** Teacher models each stage; divide the task into short steps; provide accessible text extracts, vocabulary support and planning prompts. Accept appropriate AAC, sign, oral, dictation or written response.
**Worked example:** Interpret task and audience → select relevant evidence → organise claim and explanation → draft → review accuracy, coherence and register. Teacher models with the authorised text.
**Learner response:** ________________________________________________
**Answer guidance:** Credit accurate evidence-supported responses; accept paraphrase and alternative interpretations with justification. Assess only verified taught/assessed conventions.
**Evidence / feedback:** __________________ **Official indicator/source page:** PENDING VERIFICATION **Review:** PENDING TEACHER REVIEW

**Objective:** Examine representation, perspective and omission; substantiate an interpretation with textual evidence.
**Task:** Closely examine a complex text; identify claims, structure, evidence and implications. Use the teacher-selected authorised text. Identify the central claim or idea, select one supporting detail, and explain one implication or question.
**Gold:** Extend the same objective through synthesis, qualification, comparison, counterargument or independent stylistic revision.
**Worked example:** Interpret task and audience → select relevant evidence → organise claim and explanation → draft → review accuracy, coherence and register. Teacher models with the authorised text.
**Learner response:** ________________________________________________
**Answer guidance:** Credit accurate evidence-supported responses; accept paraphrase and alternative interpretations with justification. Assess only verified taught/assessed conventions.
**Evidence / feedback:** __________________ **Official indicator/source page:** PENDING VERIFICATION **Review:** PENDING TEACHER REVIEW

**Objective:** Examine representation, perspective and omission; substantiate an interpretation with textual evidence.
**Task:** Present a reasoned position and respond thoughtfully to questions. Use the teacher-selected authorised text. Identify the central claim or idea, select one supporting detail, and explain one implication or question.
**Gold:** Extend the same objective through synthesis, qualification, comparison, counterargument or independent stylistic revision.
**Worked example:** Interpret task and audience → select relevant evidence → organise claim and explanation → draft → review accuracy, coherence and register. Teacher models with the authorised text.

**Answer guidance:** Credit accurate evidence-supported responses; accept paraphrase and alternative interpretations with justification. Assess only verified taught/assessed conventions.
